# Order Book Imbalance on Hyperliquid — Research Results

## Cost model (Hyperliquid perps)

| scenario                             |   entry_fee_bps |   exit_fee_bps |   breakeven_bps |
|:-------------------------------------|----------------:|---------------:|----------------:|
| Tier 0 limit / limit (base case)     |             1.5 |            1.5 |           3     |
| Tier 0 limit entry / market exit     |             1.5 |            4.5 |           6.003 |
| Tier 0 market / market               |             4.5 |            4.5 |           9.004 |
| Tier 0 + Gold staking, limit / limit |             1.2 |            1.2 |           2.4   |
| Tier 3 (>$100M 14d), limit / limit   |             0.4 |            0.4 |           0.8   |
| Maker rebate 0.001%, limit / limit   |            -0.1 |           -0.1 |          -0.2   |

# SOL

Order size 5.3749 SOL (1,000 USD); latency 200 ms per order; median spread 0.848 bps (last test day). Base execution: limit entry + limit exit (timeouts 5s / 5s, market fallback on exit).
